Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in optimal theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 429 and the invariant I(A,B) = 799 mod 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_429(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 429. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^799(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)
